(Lcom/tihyo/legends/management/characters/LegendsCharacter;Lnet/minecraft/entity/player/EntityPlayer;Lcom/tihyo/legends/abilities/Ability;I)Ljava/lang/Integer;
An extra-dimensional field of negative energy that eats away at the regular Speed Force like a cancer and can consume any speedster connected to it by touch. At Speed 20 or higher, the Negative Speed Force allows you to attack opponents at high velocities, able to punch your target faster than normal at a wider range. When in the Negative or regular Speed Force, damage is reduced by half while regeneration increases to 3. Extinguishes flames when at high speeds.
